lemma no_sol_n_100 (N : ℕ) (hN1 : 105 ≤ N) (hN2 : N ≤ 299) :
    ¬ ∃ k : ℕ, 1 ≤ k ∧ k ≤ N - 2 ∧ (Ico k N).sum (fun i => i ^ 100) = N ^ 100 := by
  intro h
  rcases h with ⟨k, hk1, hk2, h_sum⟩
  interval_cases N
  · interval_cases k <;> revert h_sum <;> decide
  · interval_cases k <;> revert h_sum <;> decide
